NOTEBOOK: Economic and Finance.ipynb
CELL: 29
==================================================
# ============================================================
# CELL 28 — Paired bootstrap model comparison
#
# Reviewer requirement:
#   Paired statistical comparison of Logistic vs HistGB vs MLP
#   with confidence intervals for performance differences.
#
# Uses SAME test observations and predictions from Cell 27.
# NO RETRAINING.
#
# Metrics:
#   ROC-AUC difference
#   Brier-score difference
#   Log-loss difference
#
# Bootstrap:
#   Stratified paired bootstrap
#   2,000 replicates
#   seed = 2026
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sklearn.metrics import (
    roc_auc_score,
    brier_score_loss,
    log_loss
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Load fixed test labels
# ------------------------------------------------------------

y = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell27_y_test.npy"
).astype(int)

print("=" * 110)
print("A. FIXED TEST COHORT")
print("=" * 110)

print("N:", f"{len(y):,}")
print("Defaults:", f"{y.sum():,}")
print("Non-defaults:", f"{(y == 0).sum():,}")
print("Default rate:", f"{y.mean():.6f}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. Training sizes to compare
#
# 180k = manuscript-motivated sensitivity point
# full = revised maximum-data specification
# ------------------------------------------------------------

FULL_N = 1_304_058

sizes_to_compare = [
    180_000,
    FULL_N
]

models = [
    "Logistic_SGD",
    "HistGB",
    "MLP"
]

pairs = [
    ("Logistic_SGD", "HistGB"),
    ("Logistic_SGD", "MLP"),
    ("HistGB", "MLP")
]


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Load predictions
# ------------------------------------------------------------

pred = {}

for n_train in sizes_to_compare:

    for model in models:

        path = (
            OUTPUT_DIR /
            f"cell27_{n_train}_{model}_test_pred.npy"
        )

        pred[(n_train, model)] = np.load(path)

        assert len(pred[(n_train, model)]) == len(y)


print("\nPredictions loaded successfully.")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Original point estimates
# ------------------------------------------------------------

point_rows = []

for n_train in sizes_to_compare:

    for model in models:

        p = pred[(n_train, model)]

        point_rows.append({
            "training_n": n_train,
            "model": model,
            "auc": roc_auc_score(y, p),
            "brier": brier_score_loss(y, p),
            "logloss": log_loss(
                y,
                p,
                labels=[0, 1]
            )
        })


point_df = pd.DataFrame(point_rows)

print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("B. ORIGINAL TEST PERFORMANCE")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    point_df.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "auc": "{:.6f}".format,
            "brier": "{:.6f}".format,
            "logloss": "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# E. Stratified paired bootstrap indices
#
# Same sampled observations are used for every model in each
# replicate, preserving the paired comparison.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

pos_idx = np.flatnonzero(y == 1)
neg_idx = np.flatnonzero(y == 0)

n_pos = len(pos_idx)
n_neg = len(neg_idx)

B = 2000
rng = np.random.default_rng(2026)

print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("C. PAIRED STRATIFIED BOOTSTRAP")
print("=" * 110)

print("Replicates:", B)
print("Positive observations:", n_pos)
print("Negative observations:", n_neg)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# F. Bootstrap comparisons
#
# Difference convention:
#   AUC difference    = model_A - model_B
#
#   Brier difference  = model_A - model_B
#   Log-loss difference = model_A - model_B
#
# Therefore:
#   AUC > 0 favours model A.
#   Brier/log-loss < 0 favours model A.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

boot_store = {}

for n_train in sizes_to_compare:

    for model_a, model_b in pairs:

        key = (
            n_train,
            model_a,
            model_b
        )

        boot_store[key] = {
            "auc_diff": np.empty(B),
            "brier_diff": np.empty(B),
            "logloss_diff": np.empty(B)
        }


for b in range(B):

    sampled_pos = rng.choice(
        pos_idx,
        size=n_pos,
        replace=True
    )

    sampled_neg = rng.choice(
        neg_idx,
        size=n_neg,
        replace=True
    )

    idx = np.concatenate([
        sampled_pos,
        sampled_neg
    ])

    y_b = y[idx]

    for n_train in sizes_to_compare:

        # calculate metrics once per model/replicate
        metrics_b = {}

        for model in models:

            p_b = pred[(n_train, model)][idx]

            metrics_b[model] = {
                "auc":
                    roc_auc_score(
                        y_b,
                        p_b
                    ),

                "brier":
                    brier_score_loss(
                        y_b,
                        p_b
                    ),

                "logloss":
                    log_loss(
                        y_b,
                        p_b,
                        labels=[0, 1]
                    )
            }

        for model_a, model_b in pairs:

            store = boot_store[
                (
                    n_train,
                    model_a,
                    model_b
                )
            ]

            store["auc_diff"][b] = (
                metrics_b[model_a]["auc"]
                -
                metrics_b[model_b]["auc"]
            )

            store["brier_diff"][b] = (
                metrics_b[model_a]["brier"]
                -
                metrics_b[model_b]["brier"]
            )

            store["logloss_diff"][b] = (
                metrics_b[model_a]["logloss"]
                -
                metrics_b[model_b]["logloss"]
            )

    if (b + 1) % 250 == 0:
        print(
            f"Completed {b + 1:,}/{B:,} bootstrap replicates"
        )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# G. Summarise paired differences
# ------------------------------------------------------------

summary_rows = []

for n_train in sizes_to_compare:

    for model_a, model_b in pairs:

        pa = pred[(n_train, model_a)]
        pb = pred[(n_train, model_b)]

        original_auc_diff = (
            roc_auc_score(y, pa)
            -
            roc_auc_score(y, pb)
        )

        original_brier_diff = (
            brier_score_loss(y, pa)
            -
            brier_score_loss(y, pb)
        )

        original_logloss_diff = (
            log_loss(y, pa, labels=[0, 1])
            -
            log_loss(y, pb, labels=[0, 1])
        )

        store = boot_store[
            (
                n_train,
                model_a,
                model_b
            )
        ]

        auc_low, auc_high = np.percentile(
            store["auc_diff"],
            [2.5, 97.5]
        )

        brier_low, brier_high = np.percentile(
            store["brier_diff"],
            [2.5, 97.5]
        )

        log_low, log_high = np.percentile(
            store["logloss_diff"],
            [2.5, 97.5]
        )

        # Two-sided bootstrap sign-based p-value.
        # Add-one correction prevents zero p-values.
        auc_le_zero = np.sum(
            store["auc_diff"] <= 0
        )

        auc_ge_zero = np.sum(
            store["auc_diff"] >= 0
        )

        auc_p = min(
            1.0,
            2 * min(
                (auc_le_zero + 1) / (B + 1),
                (auc_ge_zero + 1) / (B + 1)
            )
        )

        summary_rows.append({
            "training_n": n_train,
            "model_A": model_a,
            "model_B": model_b,

            "auc_diff_A_minus_B":
                original_auc_diff,
            "auc_ci_low":
                auc_low,
            "auc_ci_high":
                auc_high,
            "auc_bootstrap_p":
                auc_p,

            "brier_diff_A_minus_B":
                original_brier_diff,
            "brier_ci_low":
                brier_low,
            "brier_ci_high":
                brier_high,

            "logloss_diff_A_minus_B":
                original_logloss_diff,
            "logloss_ci_low":
                log_low,
            "logloss_ci_high":
                log_high
        })


paired_summary = pd.DataFrame(
    summary_rows
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# H. Print reviewer-facing results
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("D. PAIRED MODEL COMPARISONS")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    paired_summary.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "auc_diff_A_minus_B":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "auc_ci_low":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "auc_ci_high":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "auc_bootstrap_p":
                "{:.6f}".format,

            "brier_diff_A_minus_B":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "brier_ci_low":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "brier_ci_high":
                "{:+.6f}".format,

            "logloss_diff_A_minus_B":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "logloss_ci_low":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "logloss_ci_high":
                "{:+.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# I. Simple interpretation flags
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def ci_excludes_zero(low, high):
    return (low > 0) or (high < 0)


paired_summary[
    "auc_difference_supported"
] = paired_summary.apply(
    lambda r:
        ci_excludes_zero(
            r["auc_ci_low"],
            r["auc_ci_high"]
        ),
    axis=1
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("E. AUC DIFFERENCE FLAGS")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    paired_summary[
        [
            "training_n",
            "model_A",
            "model_B",
            "auc_diff_A_minus_B",
            "auc_ci_low",
            "auc_ci_high",
            "auc_bootstrap_p",
            "auc_difference_supported"
        ]
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "auc_diff_A_minus_B":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "auc_ci_low":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "auc_ci_high":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "auc_bootstrap_p":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# J. Save
# ------------------------------------------------------------

point_df.to_csv(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell28_test_performance_point_estimates.csv",
    index=False
)

paired_summary.to_csv(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell28_paired_bootstrap_model_comparisons.csv",
    index=False
)


print("\nSaved Cell 28 outputs to:")
print(OUTPUT_DIR)

print(
    "\nDo NOT use the phrase 'no capacity premium' "
    "until these paired results are interpreted."
)

==================================================
NOTEBOOK: Economic and Finance.ipynb
CELL: 30
==================================================
# ============================================================
# CELL 29 — Validation-only recalibration
#
# Selected model:
#   Full-training HistGB
#
# Selection basis:
#   Highest validation ROC-AUC among full-training models.
#
# Recalibration:
#   Logistic/Platt recalibration fitted ONLY on validation:
#
#   logit(Y) = a + b * logit(p_raw)
#
#   The fitted mapping is then applied unchanged to test.
#
# NO TEST INFORMATION IS USED TO FIT RECALIBRATION.
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import (
    roc_auc_score,
    brier_score_loss,
    log_loss
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Load fixed labels and selected-model predictions
# ------------------------------------------------------------

FULL_N = 1_304_058
SELECTED_MODEL = "HistGB"

y_val = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR / "cell27_y_val.npy"
).astype(int)

y_test = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR / "cell27_y_test.npy"
).astype(int)

p_val_raw = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    f"cell27_{FULL_N}_{SELECTED_MODEL}_val_pred.npy"
)

p_test_raw = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    f"cell27_{FULL_N}_{SELECTED_MODEL}_test_pred.npy"
)


print("=" * 110)
print("A. RECALIBRATION COHORTS")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    "Selected model:",
    SELECTED_MODEL
)

print(
    "Validation N:",
    f"{len(y_val):,}",
    "| event rate:",
    f"{y_val.mean():.6f}",
    "| raw mean prediction:",
    f"{p_val_raw.mean():.6f}"
)

print(
    "Test N:",
    f"{len(y_test):,}",
    "| event rate:",
    f"{y_test.mean():.6f}",
    "| raw mean prediction:",
    f"{p_test_raw.mean():.6f}"
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. Helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------

EPS = 1e-6


def clip_prob(p):
    return np.clip(
        np.asarray(p, dtype=float),
        EPS,
        1 - EPS
    )


def logit(p):
    p = clip_prob(p)
    return np.log(
        p / (1 - p)
    )


def sigmoid(z):
    return 1 / (
        1 + np.exp(-z)
    )


def calibration_intercept_slope(y, p):
    """
    Logistic calibration model:
        logit(Y) = intercept + slope * logit(p)

    Ideal:
        intercept = 0
        slope = 1
    """

    x = logit(p).reshape(-1, 1)

    model = LogisticRegression(
        penalty=None,
        solver="lbfgs",
        max_iter=2000
    )

    model.fit(
        x,
        y
    )

    return (
        float(model.intercept_[0]),
        float(model.coef_[0, 0])
    )


def ece_equal_width(y, p, n_bins=10):
    """
    Expected calibration error using 10 equal-width
    probability bins.
    """

    p = np.asarray(p)
    y = np.asarray(y)

    edges = np.linspace(
        0,
        1,
        n_bins + 1
    )

    bin_id = np.digitize(
        p,
        edges[1:-1],
        right=False
    )

    ece = 0.0

    rows = []

    for b in range(n_bins):

        mask = (
            bin_id == b
        )

        n = int(mask.sum())

        if n == 0:
            continue

        mean_p = float(
            p[mask].mean()
        )

        event_rate = float(
            y[mask].mean()
        )

        weight = (
            n / len(y)
        )

        gap = abs(
            event_rate - mean_p
        )

        ece += (
            weight * gap
        )

        rows.append({
            "bin": b + 1,
            "lower": edges[b],
            "upper": edges[b + 1],
            "n": n,
            "mean_prediction": mean_p,
            "event_rate": event_rate,
            "abs_gap": gap
        })

    return float(ece), pd.DataFrame(rows)


def calibration_metrics(y, p):

    p = clip_prob(p)

    intercept, slope = (
        calibration_intercept_slope(
            y,
            p
        )
    )

    ece, _ = ece_equal_width(
        y,
        p,
        n_bins=10
    )

    return {
        "n": len(y),
        "event_rate":
            float(np.mean(y)),
        "mean_prediction":
            float(np.mean(p)),
        "auc":
            roc_auc_score(y, p),
        "brier":
            brier_score_loss(y, p),
        "logloss":
            log_loss(
                y,
                p,
                labels=[0, 1]
            ),
        "ece":
            ece,
        "calibration_intercept":
            intercept,
        "calibration_slope":
            slope
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Fit Platt/logistic recalibration ONLY on validation
# ------------------------------------------------------------

X_cal_val = (
    logit(p_val_raw)
    .reshape(-1, 1)
)

recalibrator = LogisticRegression(
    penalty=None,
    solver="lbfgs",
    max_iter=2000
)

recalibrator.fit(
    X_cal_val,
    y_val
)

recal_intercept = float(
    recalibrator.intercept_[0]
)

recal_slope = float(
    recalibrator.coef_[0, 0]
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("B. VALIDATION-FITTED RECALIBRATION PARAMETERS")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    "Intercept a:",
    f"{recal_intercept:.6f}"
)

print(
    "Slope b:",
    f"{recal_slope:.6f}"
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Apply unchanged mapping
# ------------------------------------------------------------

p_val_cal = recalibrator.predict_proba(
    logit(p_val_raw).reshape(-1, 1)
)[:, 1]

p_test_cal = recalibrator.predict_proba(
    logit(p_test_raw).reshape(-1, 1)
)[:, 1]


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# E. Before/after metrics
# ------------------------------------------------------------

rows = []

for cohort_name, y, p_raw, p_cal in [
    (
        "Validation",
        y_val,
        p_val_raw,
        p_val_cal
    ),
    (
        "Test",
        y_test,
        p_test_raw,
        p_test_cal
    )
]:

    raw_metrics = calibration_metrics(
        y,
        p_raw
    )

    cal_metrics = calibration_metrics(
        y,
        p_cal
    )

    rows.append({
        "cohort": cohort_name,
        "stage": "Before",
        **raw_metrics
    })

    rows.append({
        "cohort": cohort_name,
        "stage": "After",
        **cal_metrics
    })


calibration_table = pd.DataFrame(
    rows
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("C. BEFORE / AFTER CALIBRATION")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    calibration_table.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "event_rate":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "mean_prediction":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "auc":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "brier":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "logloss":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "ece":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "calibration_intercept":
                "{:+.6f}".format,
            "calibration_slope":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# F. Test improvement table
# ------------------------------------------------------------

test_before = calibration_table[
    (calibration_table["cohort"] == "Test") &
    (calibration_table["stage"] == "Before")
].iloc[0]

test_after = calibration_table[
    (calibration_table["cohort"] == "Test") &
    (calibration_table["stage"] == "After")
].iloc[0]


improvement = pd.DataFrame([{
    "delta_mean_prediction":
        test_after["mean_prediction"]
        -
        test_before["mean_prediction"],

    "delta_auc":
        test_after["auc"]
        -
        test_before["auc"],

    "delta_brier":
        test_after["brier"]
        -
        test_before["brier"],

    "delta_logloss":
        test_after["logloss"]
        -
        test_before["logloss"],

    "delta_ece":
        test_after["ece"]
        -
        test_before["ece"],

    "intercept_before":
        test_before["calibration_intercept"],

    "intercept_after":
        test_after["calibration_intercept"],

    "slope_before":
        test_before["calibration_slope"],

    "slope_after":
        test_after["calibration_slope"]
}])


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("D. TEST CHANGE AFTER VALIDATION-ONLY RECALIBRATION")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    improvement.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            c: "{:+.6f}".format
            for c in improvement.columns
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# G. Reliability-bin tables
# ------------------------------------------------------------

_, val_before_bins = ece_equal_width(
    y_val,
    p_val_raw
)

_, val_after_bins = ece_equal_width(
    y_val,
    p_val_cal
)

_, test_before_bins = ece_equal_width(
    y_test,
    p_test_raw
)

_, test_after_bins = ece_equal_width(
    y_test,
    p_test_cal
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("E. TEST RELIABILITY BINS — BEFORE")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    test_before_bins.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "lower": "{:.2f}".format,
            "upper": "{:.2f}".format,
            "mean_prediction":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "event_rate":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "abs_gap":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("F. TEST RELIABILITY BINS — AFTER")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    test_after_bins.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "lower": "{:.2f}".format,
            "upper": "{:.2f}".format,
            "mean_prediction":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "event_rate":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "abs_gap":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# H. Save probabilities for threshold analysis
# ------------------------------------------------------------

np.save(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell29_histgb_full_val_raw.npy",
    p_val_raw
)

np.save(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell29_histgb_full_val_recalibrated.npy",
    p_val_cal
)

np.save(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell29_histgb_full_test_raw.npy",
    p_test_raw

# ============================================================
# CELL 30 — Recalibrated threshold / decision analysis
#
# Thresholds are selected ONLY on the validation cohort using
# validation-recalibrated HistGB probabilities.
#
# The selected threshold is then applied unchanged to test.
#
# Cost convention:
#   FP cost = 1
#   FN cost = {5, 10, 20}
#
# Review/flag:
#   predicted probability >= threshold
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Load recalibrated probabilities and labels
# ------------------------------------------------------------

y_val = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR / "cell27_y_val.npy"
).astype(int)

y_test = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR / "cell27_y_test.npy"
).astype(int)

p_val = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell29_histgb_full_val_recalibrated.npy"
)

p_test = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell29_histgb_full_test_recalibrated.npy"
)


print("=" * 110)
print("A. INPUT CHECK")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    "Validation:",
    f"N={len(y_val):,}",
    f"default rate={y_val.mean():.6f}",
    f"mean prediction={p_val.mean():.6f}"
)

print(
    "Test:",
    f"N={len(y_test):,}",
    f"default rate={y_test.mean():.6f}",
    f"mean prediction={p_test.mean():.6f}"
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. Decision metrics
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def decision_metrics(y, p, threshold, fn_cost, fp_cost=1.0):

    flagged = p >= threshold

    tp = int(
        np.sum(
            flagged & (y == 1)
        )
    )

    fp = int(
        np.sum(
            flagged & (y == 0)
        )
    )

    tn = int(
        np.sum(
            (~flagged) & (y == 0)
        )
    )

    fn = int(
        np.sum(
            (~flagged) & (y == 1)
        )
    )

    n = len(y)

    positives = (
        tp + fn
    )

    flagged_n = (
        tp + fp
    )

    fnr = (
        fn / positives
        if positives > 0
        else np.nan
    )

    tpr = (
        tp / positives
        if positives > 0
        else np.nan
    )

    review_rate = (
        flagged_n / n
    )

    precision = (
        tp / flagged_n
        if flagged_n > 0
        else np.nan
    )

    cost = (
        fn_cost * fn
        +
        fp_cost * fp
    )

    no_review_cost = (
        fn_cost
        * positives
    )

    net_benefit = (
        no_review_cost
        -
        cost
    )

    return {
        "threshold": threshold,
        "tp": tp,
        "fp": fp,
        "tn": tn,
        "fn": fn,
        "fnr": fnr,
        "tpr": tpr,
        "review_rate": review_rate,
        "precision": precision,
        "cost": cost,
        "no_review_cost": no_review_cost,
        "net_benefit": net_benefit
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Exact validation threshold search
#
# For a threshold classifier, predictions change only when the
# threshold crosses an observed predicted probability.
#
# We therefore evaluate all unique validation probabilities,
# plus boundary operating points.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def find_cost_minimising_threshold(
    y,
    p,
    fn_cost,
    fp_cost=1.0
):

    order = np.argsort(
        -p,
        kind="mergesort"
    )

    p_sorted = p[order]
    y_sorted = y[order]

    total_pos = int(
        y_sorted.sum()
    )

    total_neg = int(
        len(y_sorted)
        -
        total_pos
    )

    cum_tp = np.cumsum(
        y_sorted == 1
    )

    cum_fp = np.cumsum(
        y_sorted == 0
    )

    # Last position for each unique probability
    is_last_of_value = np.r_[
        p_sorted[:-1] != p_sorted[1:],
        True
    ]

    positions = np.flatnonzero(
        is_last_of_value
    )

    thresholds = p_sorted[
        positions
    ]

    tp = cum_tp[
        positions
    ]

    fp = cum_fp[
        positions
    ]

    fn = (
        total_pos
        -
        tp
    )

    costs = (
        fn_cost * fn
        +
        fp_cost * fp
    )

    best_cost = costs.min()

    best_positions = np.flatnonzero(
        costs == best_cost
    )

    # Tie rule:
    # choose the HIGHEST threshold among equal-cost solutions,
    # i.e. the lower review burden.
    best_local = best_positions[
        np.argmax(
            thresholds[
                best_positions
            ]
        )
    ]

    best_threshold = float(
        thresholds[
            best_local
        ]
    )

    return (
        best_threshold,
        float(best_cost)
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Select threshold on validation, evaluate on both cohorts
# ------------------------------------------------------------

COST_RATIOS = [
    5,
    10,
    20
]

rows = []


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("B. VALIDATION-SELECTED RECALIBRATED THRESHOLDS")
print("=" * 110)


for fn_cost in COST_RATIOS:

    threshold, validation_min_cost = (
        find_cost_minimising_threshold(
            y_val,
            p_val,
            fn_cost=fn_cost,
            fp_cost=1.0
        )
    )

    val_metrics = decision_metrics(
        y_val,
        p_val,
        threshold,
        fn_cost=fn_cost
    )

    test_metrics = decision_metrics(
        y_test,
        p_test,
        threshold,
        fn_cost=fn_cost
    )

    print(
        f"\nFN:FP = {fn_cost}:1"
    )

    print(
        f"  Selected validation threshold: "
        f"{threshold:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Validation FNR: "
        f"{val_metrics['fnr']:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Validation review rate: "
        f"{val_metrics['review_rate']:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Test FNR: "
        f"{test_metrics['fnr']:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Test review rate: "
        f"{test_metrics['review_rate']:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Test precision: "
        f"{test_metrics['precision']:.6f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Test cost: "
        f"{test_metrics['cost']:.1f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Test no-review cost: "
        f"{test_metrics['no_review_cost']:.1f}"
    )

    print(
        f"  Test net benefit: "
        f"{test_metrics['net_benefit']:.1f}"
    )

    for cohort, metrics in [
        ("Validation", val_metrics),
        ("Test", test_metrics)
    ]:

        rows.append({
            "fn_fp_ratio":
                f"{fn_cost}:1",
            "fn_cost":
                fn_cost,
            "fp_cost":
                1.0,
            "threshold":
                threshold,
            "threshold_selected_on":
                "Validation",
            "cohort":
                cohort,
            **{
                k: v
                for k, v in metrics.items()
                if k != "threshold"
            }
        })


threshold_results = pd.DataFrame(
    rows
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# E. Reviewer-facing test table
# ------------------------------------------------------------

test_threshold_table = (
    threshold_results[
        threshold_results["cohort"]
        == "Test"
    ]
    .copy()
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("C. TEST PERFORMANCE AT VALIDATION-SELECTED THRESHOLDS")
print("=" * 110)

display_cols = [
    "fn_fp_ratio",
    "threshold",
    "tp",
    "fp",
    "tn",
    "fn",
    "fnr",
    "review_rate",
    "precision",
    "cost",
    "no_review_cost",
    "net_benefit"
]

print(
    test_threshold_table[
        display_cols
    ].to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "threshold":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "fnr":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "review_rate":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "precision":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "cost":
                "{:.1f}".format,
            "no_review_cost":
                "{:.1f}".format,
            "net_benefit":
                "{:.1f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# F. Compare with ORIGINAL manuscript operating points
#
# These are for descriptive comparison only.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

old_results = pd.DataFrame({
    "fn_fp_ratio": [
        "5:1",
        "10:1",
        "20:1"
    ],
    "old_threshold": [
        0.1984,

        0.0292
    ],

        0.9695,
        0.3786,

    ],
    "old_review_rate": [
        0.0129,
        0.4034,
        0.7822
    ],
    "old_precision": [
        0.3465,
        0.2256,
        0.1724
    ]
})


comparison = (
    test_threshold_table[
        [
            "fn_fp_ratio",
            "threshold",
            "fnr",
            "review_rate",
            "precision"
        ]
    ]
    .rename(
        columns={
            "threshold":
                "new_threshold",
            "fnr":
                "new_test_fnr",
            "review_rate":
                "new_review_rate",
            "precision":
                "new_precision"
        }
    )
    .merge(
        old_results,
        on="fn_fp_ratio",
        how="left"
    )
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("D. OLD MANUSCRIPT vs RECALIBRATED ANALYSIS")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    comparison.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "new_threshold":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "new_test_fnr":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "new_review_rate":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "new_precision":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "old_threshold":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "old_test_fnr":
                "{:.6f}".format,

                "{:.6f}".format,

        }

# ------------------------------------------------------------

threshold_results.to_csv(

# ============================================================
# CELL 31A — FINAL BLOCK, PART 1
# Stress-definition provenance audit
#
# Purpose:
#   Resolve the manuscript claim:
#       "stress = national unemployment >= 4.90%"
#   and determine what actually produces the historical
#   stress sample of approximately n = 641.
#
# NO MODEL TRAINING.
# NO NEW STRESS DEFINITION IS INVENTED HERE.
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Test cohort used in revised analysis
# ------------------------------------------------------------

test_mask = (
    (df_model["split_revised"] == "test")
    &
    (df_model["mature_reconstructed"])
)

test_idx = df_model.index[test_mask]

print("=" * 110)
print("A. FIXED MATURE TEST COHORT")
print("=" * 110)

print("Test N:", f"{len(test_idx):,}")

print(
    "Expected:",
    "41,145",
    "|",
    "MATCH" if len(test_idx) == 41145 else "DOES NOT MATCH"
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. Read only stress-relevant raw variables
# ------------------------------------------------------------

stress_cols = [
    "issue_d",
    "issue_date",
    "addr_state",
    "state",
    "region",
    "subregion",
    "unemployment",
    "unem_rate",
    "logunemployment",
    "FEDFUNDS",
    "earnings",
    "employment",
    "labor_force",
    "gdpindex",
    "gdpreal",
    "gdppercap",
    "cpi",
    "CPIUS",
    "inf",
    "infUS"
]

stress_raw = pd.read_stata(
    DTA_PATH,
    columns=stress_cols,
    convert_categoricals=False
)

stress_test = stress_raw.loc[
    test_idx
].copy()

print("\nStress-variable frame:",
      stress_test.shape)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Basic distributions
# ------------------------------------------------------------

audit_vars = [
    "unemployment",
    "unem_rate",
    "logunemployment",
    "FEDFUNDS"
]

audit_rows = []

for c in audit_vars:

    s = pd.to_numeric(
        stress_test[c],
        errors="coerce"
    )

    audit_rows.append({
        "variable": c,
        "n": s.notna().sum(),
        "missing_pct":
            100 * s.isna().mean(),
        "min": s.min(),
        "p01": s.quantile(.01),
        "p25": s.quantile(.25),
        "median": s.quantile(.50),
        "p75": s.quantile(.75),
        "p90": s.quantile(.90),
        "p95": s.quantile(.95),
        "p99": s.quantile(.99),
        "max": s.max(),
        "nunique": s.nunique()
    })

stress_distribution = pd.DataFrame(
    audit_rows
)

print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("B. STRESS-VARIABLE DISTRIBUTIONS — MATURE TEST")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    stress_distribution.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            c: "{:.6f}".format
            for c in [
                "missing_pct",
                "min",
                "p01",
                "p25",
                "median",
                "p75",
                "p90",
                "p95",
                "p99",
                "max"
            ]
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Unique unemployment values and counts
# ------------------------------------------------------------

for c in [
    "unem_rate",
    "unemployment"
]:

    print("\n" + "=" * 110)
    print(
        f"C. MOST COMMON VALUES: {c}"
    )
    print("=" * 110)

    tmp = (
        stress_test[c]
        .value_counts(
            dropna=False
        )
        .head(30)
    )

    print(tmp.to_string())


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# E. Monthly structure
# ------------------------------------------------------------

stress_test[
    "issue_month_clean"
] = pd.to_datetime(
    stress_test["issue_d"]
        .astype(str)
        .str.strip(),
    format="%b-%y",
    errors="coerce"
)

monthly = (
    stress_test
    .groupby(
        "issue_month_clean",
        dropna=False
    )
    .agg(
        n=("issue_d", "size"),

        unemployment_min=(
            "unemployment",
            "min"
        ),
        unemployment_median=(
            "unemployment",
            "median"
        ),
        unemployment_max=(
            "unemployment",
            "max"
        ),

        unem_rate_min=(
            "unem_rate",
            "min"
        ),
        unem_rate_median=(
            "unem_rate",
            "median"
        ),
        unem_rate_max=(
            "unem_rate",
            "max"
        )
    )
    .reset_index()
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("D. MONTHLY UNEMPLOYMENT STRUCTURE")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    monthly.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "unemployment_min":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unemployment_median":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unemployment_max":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unem_rate_min":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unem_rate_median":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unem_rate_max":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# F. Explicit 4.90% threshold audit
#
# Test both possible scales:
#   >= 4.90
#   >= 0.049
#
# We do NOT assume which scale is correct.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

threshold_rows = []

for variable in [
    "unemployment",
    "unem_rate"
]:

    s = pd.to_numeric(
        stress_test[variable],
        errors="coerce"
    )

    for threshold in [
        4.90,
        0.049
    ]:

        mask = (
            s >= threshold
        )

        threshold_rows.append({
            "variable":
                variable,
            "threshold":
                threshold,
            "stress_n":
                int(mask.sum()),
            "stress_pct":
                100 * mask.mean()
        })


threshold_audit = pd.DataFrame(
    threshold_rows
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("E. EXPLICIT 4.90% THRESHOLD AUDIT")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    threshold_audit.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "threshold":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "stress_pct":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# G. Search thresholds that produce counts near historical 641
#
# This is DIAGNOSTIC ONLY.
# It does not define stress.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

target_n = 641

near_rows = []

for variable in [
    "unemployment",
    "unem_rate"
]:

    s = pd.to_numeric(
        stress_test[variable],
        errors="coerce"
    ).dropna()

    unique_vals = np.sort(
        s.unique()
    )

    for threshold in unique_vals:

        n_ge = int(
            (s >= threshold).sum()
        )

        if abs(
            n_ge - target_n
        ) <= 100:

            near_rows.append({
                "variable":
                    variable,
                "threshold":
                    threshold,
                "n_ge_threshold":
                    n_ge,
                "difference_from_641":
                    n_ge - target_n
            })


near_641 = pd.DataFrame(
    near_rows
)

if len(near_641):

    near_641 = (
        near_641
        .sort_values(
            [
                "difference_from_641",
                "variable"
            ],
            key=lambda x:
                np.abs(x)
                if x.name == "difference_from_641"
                else x
        )
    )


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("F. THRESHOLDS PRODUCING COUNTS NEAR n=641")
print("=" * 110)

if len(near_641):

    print(
        near_641.to_string(
            index=False,
            formatters={
                "threshold":
                    "{:.8f}".format
            }
        )
    )

else:

    print(
        "No unemployment/unem_rate threshold "
        "produces a mature-test count within ±100 of 641."
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# H. Quantile-based diagnostic
#
# Old notebook reportedly used approximately the upper quartile
# of unem_rate in at least one exploratory pipeline.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("G. QUANTILE DIAGNOSTIC")
print("=" * 110)

for variable in [
    "unemployment",
    "unem_rate"
]:

    s = pd.to_numeric(
        stress_test[variable],
        errors="coerce"
    )

    for q in [
        .75,
        .90,
        .95,
        .975,
        .99
    ]:

        threshold = s.quantile(q)

        n_ge = int(
            (s >= threshold).sum()
        )

        print(
            f"{variable:15s} "
            f"q={q:.3f} "
            f"threshold={threshold:.8f} "
            f"n>={n_ge:,}"
        )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# I. Relationship between unemployment variables
# ------------------------------------------------------------

corr_cols = [
    "unemployment",
    "unem_rate",
    "logunemployment"
]

corr_df = (
    stress_test[corr_cols]
    .apply(
        pd.to_numeric,
        errors="coerce"
    )
    .corr()
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("H. CORRELATION BETWEEN UNEMPLOYMENT VARIABLES")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    corr_df.to_string(
        float_format=lambda x:
            f"{x:.6f}"
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# J. State/geographic variation diagnostic
# ------------------------------------------------------------

geo_summary = (
    stress_test
    .groupby(
        "addr_state",
        dropna=False
    )
    .agg(
        n=("issue_d", "size"),
        unemployment_mean=(
            "unemployment",
            "mean"
        ),
        unemployment_min=(
            "unemployment",
            "min"
        ),
        unemployment_max=(
            "unemployment",
            "max"
        ),
        unem_rate_mean=(
            "unem_rate",
            "mean"
        ),
        unem_rate_min=(
            "unem_rate",
            "min"
        ),
        unem_rate_max=(
            "unem_rate",
            "max"
        )
    )
    .reset_index()
    .sort_values(
        "n",
        ascending=False
    )
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("I. GEOGRAPHIC VARIATION — FIRST 25 STATES")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    geo_summary
    .head(25)
    .to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "unemployment_mean":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unemployment_min":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unemployment_max":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unem_rate_mean":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unem_rate_min":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "unem_rate_max":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# K. Save outputs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

stress_distribution.to_csv(
    OUTPUT_DIR /

)

)


print("\nSaved Cell 31A outputs to:")
print(OUTPUT_DIR)

print(
    "\nIMPORTANT: Do not redefine stress yet."
)

    "stress/slice analysis."

# ============================================================
# CELL 31B — FINAL CODE CELL OF REVISION ANALYSIS
#
# Final stress + slice robustness analysis
#
# VERIFIED STRESS VARIABLE:
#   unem_rate is state-level, not national.
#
# High-unemployment subgroup:
#   unem_rate >= 0.049  (4.9%)
#
# IMPORTANT:
#   This is interpreted as a high-state-unemployment subgroup,
#   NOT as a national macroeconomic stress period.
#
# Threshold analysis:
#   Uses the validation-selected recalibrated 10:1 threshold
#   from Cell 30.
#
# Slice support rule:
#   >=10 defaults AND >=10 non-defaults in BOTH unemployment
#   regimes within the same grade x term cell.
#
# NO MODEL TRAINING.
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sklearn.metrics import (
    roc_auc_score,
    brier_score_loss,
    log_loss
)

from statsmodels.stats.proportion import proportion_confint


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Load fixed test labels and recalibrated probabilities
# ------------------------------------------------------------

y_test = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR / "cell27_y_test.npy"
).astype(int)

p_test = np.load(
    OUTPUT_DIR /
    "cell29_histgb_full_test_recalibrated.npy"
)

assert len(y_test) == 41145
assert len(p_test) == 41145


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. Reconstruct fixed test metadata in identical row order
# ------------------------------------------------------------

test_meta = stress_test.copy()

assert len(test_meta) == len(y_test)

test_meta["y"] = y_test
test_meta["p"] = p_test

test_meta["unem_rate_num"] = pd.to_numeric(
    test_meta["unem_rate"],
    errors="coerce"
)

test_meta["high_unemployment"] = (
    test_meta["unem_rate_num"] >= 0.049
)


print("=" * 110)
print("A. VERIFIED HIGH-UNEMPLOYMENT SUBGROUP")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    "Definition: state-level unemployment rate >= 4.9%"
)

print(
    "High-unemployment N:",
    f"{test_meta['high_unemployment'].sum():,}"
)

print(
    "Other-state N:",
    f"{(~test_meta['high_unemployment']).sum():,}"
)

print(
    "High-unemployment share:",
    f"{test_meta['high_unemployment'].mean():.6f}"
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Group-level descriptive metrics
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def ece_equal_width_local(y, p, n_bins=10):

    edges = np.linspace(
        0,
        1,
        n_bins + 1
    )

    ids = np.digitize(
        p,
        edges[1:-1],
        right=False
    )

    ece = 0.0

    for b in range(n_bins):

        mask = ids == b

        if mask.sum() == 0:
            continue

        ece += (
            mask.mean()
            *
            abs(
                y[mask].mean()
                -
                p[mask].mean()
            )
        )

    return float(ece)


def group_metrics(frame):

    y = frame["y"].to_numpy()
    p = frame["p"].to_numpy()

    result = {
        "n": len(frame),
        "defaults": int(y.sum()),
        "nondefaults": int(len(y) - y.sum()),
        "default_rate": float(y.mean()),
        "mean_prediction": float(p.mean()),
        "brier": float(
            brier_score_loss(y, p)
        ),
        "logloss": float(
            log_loss(
                y,
                p,
                labels=[0, 1]
            )
        ),
        "ece": ece_equal_width_local(
            y,
            p
        )
    }

    if len(np.unique(y)) == 2:
        result["auc"] = float(
            roc_auc_score(y, p)
        )
    else:
        result["auc"] = np.nan

    return result


group_rows = []

for name, mask in [
    (
        "Other-state",
        ~test_meta["high_unemployment"]
    ),
    (
        "High-unemployment",
        test_meta["high_unemployment"]
    )
]:

    metrics = group_metrics(
        test_meta.loc[mask]
    )

    group_rows.append({
        "group": name,
        **metrics
    })


group_table = pd.DataFrame(
    group_rows
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("B. GROUP PERFORMANCE")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    group_table.to_string(
        index=False,
        formatters={
            "default_rate":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "mean_prediction":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "auc":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "brier":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "logloss":
                "{:.6f}".format,
            "ece":
                "{:.6f}".format
        }
    )
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Stratified bootstrap CIs within each group
# ------------------------------------------------------------

BOOT = 2000
BOOT_SEED = 2026

rng = np.random.default_rng(
    BOOT_SEED
)


def bootstrap_group(frame, n_boot=2000):

    y = frame["y"].to_numpy()
    p = frame["p"].to_numpy()

    pos = np.flatnonzero(
        y == 1
    )

    neg = np.flatnonzero(
        y == 0
    )

    rows = []

    for b in range(n_boot):

        sampled_pos = rng.choice(
            pos,
            size=len(pos),
            replace=True
        )

        sampled_neg = rng.choice(
            neg,
            size=len(neg),
            replace=True
        )

        idx = np.concatenate(
            [
                sampled_pos,
                sampled_neg
            ]
        )

        yb = y[idx]
        pb = p[idx]

        rows.append([
            roc_auc_score(
                yb,
                pb
            ),
            brier_score_loss(
                yb,
                pb
            ),
            log_loss(
                yb,
                pb,
                labels=[0, 1]
            ),
            ece_equal_width_local(
                yb,
                pb
            )
        ])

    arr = np.asarray(
        rows
    )

    return {
        "auc_ci_low":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 0], .025),
        "auc_ci_high":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 0], .975),

        "brier_ci_low":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 1], .025),
        "brier_ci_high":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 1], .975),

        "logloss_ci_low":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 2], .025),
        "logloss_ci_high":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 2], .975),

        "ece_ci_low":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 3], .025),
        "ece_ci_high":
            np.quantile(arr[:, 3], .975)
    }


ci_rows = []

for name, mask in [
    (
        "Other-state",
        ~test_meta["high_unemployment"]
    ),
    (
        "High-unemployment",
        test_meta["high_unemployment"]
    )
]:

    print(
        f"\nBootstrapping {name}..."
    )

    ci = bootstrap_group(
        test_meta.loc[mask],
        n_boot=BOOT
    )

    ci_rows.append({
        "group": name,
        **ci
    })


ci_table = pd.DataFrame(
    ci_rows
)

group_table_ci = (
    group_table
    .merge(
        ci_table,
        on="group",
        how="left"
    )
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("C. GROUP PERFORMANCE WITH 95% BOOTSTRAP CIs")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    group_table_ci.to_string(
        index=False,
        float_format=lambda x:
            f"{x:.6f}"
    )
)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# F. Decision metrics by unemployment group
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def threshold_metrics(
    frame,
    threshold
):

    y = frame["y"].to_numpy()
    p = frame["p"].to_numpy()

    flag = (
        p >= threshold
    )

    tp = int(
        np.sum(
            flag & (y == 1)
        )
    )

    fp = int(
        np.sum(
            flag & (y == 0)
        )
    )

    tn = int(
        np.sum(
            (~flag) & (y == 0)
        )
    )

    fn = int(
        np.sum(
            (~flag) & (y == 1)
        )
    )

    defaults = (
        tp + fn
    )

    flagged = (
        tp + fp
    )

    fnr = (
        fn / defaults
        if defaults
        else np.nan
    )

    precision = (
        tp / flagged
        if flagged
        else np.nan
    )

    review_rate = (
        flagged / len(frame)
    )

    # Wilson CI for FNR
    if defaults > 0:

        fnr_low, fnr_high = (
            proportion_confint(
                fn,
                defaults,
                alpha=.05,
                method="wilson"
            )
        )

    else:

        fnr_low = np.nan
        fnr_high = np.nan

    return {
        "n": len(frame),
        "defaults": defaults,
        "tp": tp,
        "fp": fp,
        "tn": tn,
        "fn": fn,
        "fnr": fnr,
        "fnr_ci_low": fnr_low,
        "fnr_ci_high": fnr_high,
        "review_rate": review_rate,
        "precision": precision
    }


decision_rows = []

for name, mask in [
    (
        "Other-state",
        ~test_meta["high_unemployment"]
    ),
    (
        "High-unemployment",
        test_meta["high_unemployment"]
    )
]:

    m = threshold_metrics(
        test_meta.loc[mask],
        threshold_10
    )

    decision_rows.append({
        "group": name,
        **m
    })


decision_table = pd.DataFrame(
    decision_rows
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("E. 10:1 THRESHOLD PERFORMANCE BY UNEMPLOYMENT GROUP")

)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# H. Cell support audit

support_rows = []

for (
    grade,
    term
), cell in test_meta.groupby(
    [
        "grade_slice",
        "term_slice"
    ]
):

    for regime_name, regime_mask in [
        (
            "Other-state",
            ~cell["high_unemployment"]
        ),
        (
            "High-unemployment",
            cell["high_unemployment"]
        )
    ]:

        sub = cell.loc[
            regime_mask
        ]

        defaults = int(
            sub["y"].sum()
        )

        nondefaults = int(
            len(sub)
            -
            defaults
        )

        support_rows.append({
            "grade":
                grade,
            "term":
                term,
            "regime":
                regime_name,
            "n":
                len(sub),
            "defaults":
                defaults,
            "nondefaults":
                nondefaults
        })


support_table = pd.DataFrame(
    support_rows
)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# I. Apply strengthened support rule
#
# Require in BOTH regimes:
#   >=10 defaults
#   >=10 non-defaults
# ------------------------------------------------------------

support_pivot = (
    support_table
    .pivot_table(
        index=[
            "grade",
            "term"
        ],
        columns="regime",
        values=[
            "n",
            "defaults",
            "nondefaults"
        ],
        aggfunc="first",
        fill_value=0
    )
)


eligible_cells = []

for idx, row in support_pivot.iterrows():

    try:

        ok = (
            row[
                ("defaults",
                 "High-unemployment")
            ] >= 10
            and
            row[
                ("nondefaults",
                 "High-unemployment")
            ] >= 10
            and
            row[
                ("defaults",
                 "Other-state")
            ] >= 10
            and
            row[
                ("nondefaults",
                 "Other-state")
            ] >= 10
        )

    except KeyError:

        ok = False

    if ok:
        eligible_cells.append(
            idx
        )


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("F. STRENGTHENED SLICE SUPPORT AUDIT")
print("=" * 110)

print(
    "Rule: >=10 defaults and >=10 non-defaults "
    "in BOTH unemployment regimes."
)

print(
    "Eligible grade x term cells:",
    len(eligible_cells)
)

if eligible_cells:
    print(
        "Eligible cells:",
        eligible_cells
    )
else:
    print(
        "No grade x term cell satisfies the strengthened "
        "minimum-event rule."
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# J. Supported slice-level threshold diagnostics
# ------------------------------------------------------------

slice_rows = []

for grade, term in eligible_cells:

    cell = test_meta.loc[
        (
            test_meta["grade_slice"]
            == grade
        )
        &
        (
            test_meta["term_slice"]
            == term
        )
    ]

    for regime_name, regime_mask in [
        (
            "Other-state",
            ~cell["high_unemployment"]
        ),
        (
            "High-unemployment",
            cell["high_unemployment"]
        )
    ]:

        sub = cell.loc[
            regime_mask
        ]

        metrics = threshold_metrics(
            sub,
            threshold_10
        )

        slice_rows.append({
            "grade":
                grade,
            "term":
                term,
            "regime":
                regime_name,
            **metrics
        })


slice_results = pd.DataFrame(
    slice_rows
)


print("\n" + "=" * 110)
print("G. SUPPORTED GRADE x TERM SLICE RESULTS")
print("=" * 110)

if len(slice_results):

    print(
        slice_results.to_string(
            index=False,
            formatters={
                "fnr":
                    "{:.6f}".format,
                "fnr_ci_low":
                    "{:.6f}".format,
                "fnr_ci_high":
                    "{:.6f}".format,
                "review_rate":
                    "{:.6f}".format,
                "precision":
                    "{:.6f}".format
            }
        )
    )

else:

    print(
        "No supported slice-level estimates remain "
        "under the strengthened event-count rule."
    )

# This allows us to explain why cells were excluded.

    support_table[
        support_table["regime"]
        == "High-unemployment"
    ]
    .sort_values(
        [
            "defaults",
            "n"
        ],
        ascending=False
    )
)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

DATE_COLS = ["issue_date", "daily_date", "last_date", "issue_daily"]

def to_datetime_safe(df, cols=DATE_COLS):
    df = df.copy()
    for c in cols:
        if c in df.columns:
            df[c] = pd.to_datetime(df[c], errors="coerce")
    return df

df = to_datetime_safe(df)
print(df.dtypes)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

TARGET = "badloan"
DATE_COL = "issue_date"

# 1) Pick only the columns you will use
feature_cols = borrower_features + loan_features + credit_features + macro_features + time_features
df_model = df[feature_cols + [TARGET, DATE_COL]].copy()

# 2) Ensure correct dtypes
df_model[DATE_COL] = pd.to_datetime(df_model[DATE_COL], errors="coerce")
df_model[TARGET] = pd.to_numeric(df_model[TARGET], errors="coerce")

# 3) Keep valid rows
df_model = df_model.dropna(subset=[DATE_COL, TARGET])
df_model[TARGET] = (df_model[TARGET] > 0).astype(int)

# 4) Identify numeric vs categorical
cat_cols = df_model[feature_cols].select_dtypes(include=["object", "category"]).columns.tolist()
num_cols = [c for c in feature_cols if c not in cat_cols]
df_model[num_cols] = df_model[num_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce")

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

def ensure_datetime_cols(df, date_cols):
    df = df.copy()
    for c in date_cols:
        if c in df.columns:
            df[c] = pd.to_datetime(df[c], errors="coerce")
    return df

date_cols = ["issue_date", "daily_date", "last_date", "issue_daily"]
df = ensure_datetime_cols(df, date_cols)

# Create time controls from issue_date (preferred)
if "issue_date" in df.columns:
    df["issue_year"] = df["issue_date"].dt.year.astype("float32")
    df["month"]      = df["issue_date"].dt.month.astype("float32")
    df["quarter"]    = df["issue_date"].dt.quarter.astype("float32")

NOTEBOOK: Fintech_5_11_2026.ipynb
CELL: 46
==================================================
def add_feature_block(df):
    df = df.copy()

    feats = {}

    # -------------------------
    # A) Log transforms (log1p)
    # -------------------------
    log_candidates = [
        "loan_amnt", "funded_amnt", "funded_amnt_inv", "annual_inc",
        "dti", "revol_bal", "tot_hi_cred_lim", "total_bc_limit",
        "gdppercap", "unemployment", "gdpreal", "riskprem", "loan_vol", "loan_sum"
    ]
    for c in log_candidates:
        if c in df.columns:
            x = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0)
            x = x.clip(lower=0)  # safety for weird negatives
            feats[f"log_{c}"] = np.log1p(x).astype("float32")

    # -------------------------
    # B) Stress regime (binary)
    # -------------------------
    # simple, defensible baseline: upper quartile of unemployment rate
    if "unem_rate" in df.columns:
        thr = df["unem_rate"].quantile(0.75)
        feats["stress_regime"] = (df["unem_rate"] >= thr).astype("int8")

    # -------------------------
    # C) Affordability ratios
    # -------------------------
    if {"installment", "annual_inc"}.issubset(df.columns):
        denom = (df["annual_inc"].astype(float) / 12.0).replace(0, np.nan)
        feats["installment_to_monthly_inc"] = (df["installment"].astype(float) / denom).fillna(0).astype("float32")

    if {"loan_amnt", "annual_inc"}.issubset(df.columns):
        denom = df["annual_inc"].astype(float).replace(0, np.nan)
        feats["loanamt_to_annual_inc"] = (df["loan_amnt"].astype(float) / denom).fillna(0).astype("float32")

    # -------------------------
    # D) Macro change features (monthly)
    # requires time order; use issue_date if available, else daily_date
    # -------------------------
    time_col = None
    if "issue_date" in df.columns:
        time_col = "issue_date"
    elif "daily_date" in df.columns:
        time_col = "daily_date"

    if time_col is not None:
        df_sorted = df.sort_values(time_col)

        # month-to-month change (simple, interpretable)
        for c in ["unem_rate", "riskprem", "FEDFUNDS", "inf", "cpi", "gdpindex"]:
            if c in df.columns:
                chg = df_sorted[c].astype(float).diff().fillna(0)
                feats[f"{c}_chg1"] = chg.reindex(df_sorted.index).reindex(df.index).astype("float32")

    # Add engineered block at once (prevents fragmentation)
    feat_df = pd.DataFrame(feats, index=df.index)
    df = pd.concat([df, feat_df], axis=1)

    return df

df = add_feature_block(df)

# ============================================
# Time-aware Train–Validation–Test Design
# (NO model training here)
# - chronological split by issue_date (or issue_daily)
# - optional: keep stress_regime + report regime/base-rate shift
# - outputs: train_df, val_df, test_df + split summary
# ============================================

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# -----------------------------
# 0) Helpers
# -----------------------------
def ensure_datetime(df: pd.DataFrame, date_cols=("issue_date", "issue_daily", "daily_date")) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = df.copy()
    for c in date_cols:
        if c in df.columns:
            df[c] = pd.to_datetime(df[c], errors="coerce")
    return df

def drop_duplicate_columns(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    # keeps first occurrence of each column name
    return df.loc[:, ~df.columns.duplicated()].copy()

def pick_time_col(df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
    for c in ["issue_date", "issue_daily", "daily_date"]:
        if c in df.columns:
            if pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(df[c]):
                return c
    raise ValueError("No usable datetime column found. Expected one of: issue_date, issue_daily, daily_date")

def add_stress_regime(df: pd.DataFrame, unem_col="unem_rate", q=0.75, out_col="stress_regime"):
    """
    Creates stress_regime using unemployment rate threshold at q-quantile.
    Returns: df (copy), threshold value
    """
    df = df.copy()
    if unem_col not in df.columns:
        raise ValueError(f"Column '{unem_col}' not found. Cannot create stress regime.")
    thr = float(df[unem_col].quantile(q))
    df[out_col] = (df[unem_col] >= thr).astype("int8")
    return df, thr

def summarize_split(df, y_col="badloan", regime_col="stress_regime"):
    out = {
        "n_rows": len(df),
        "date_min": df["_time_"].min(),
        "date_max": df["_time_"].max(),
    }
    if y_col in df.columns:
        out["target_rate"] = float(df[y_col].mean())
    if regime_col in df.columns:
        out["stress_share"] = float(df[regime_col].mean())
        if y_col in df.columns:
            out["default_nonstress"] = float(df.loc[df[regime_col]==0, y_col].mean())
            out["default_stress"]    = float(df.loc[df[regime_col]==1, y_col].mean())
    return out

# -----------------------------
# 1) Prepare df (types + dedupe)
# -----------------------------
df = ensure_datetime(df)
df = drop_duplicate_columns(df)

time_col = pick_time_col(df)

# Keep only rows with a valid time
df = df.dropna(subset=[time_col]).copy()
df = df.sort_values(time_col).reset_index(drop=True)
df["_time_"] = df[time_col]  # internal column used for split

# -----------------------------
# 2) OPTIONAL: define stress regime (if not already present)
# -----------------------------
if "stress_regime" not in df.columns:
    df, thr75 = add_stress_regime(df, unem_col="unem_rate", q=0.75, out_col="stress_regime")
    print(f"unem_rate 75th percentile threshold: {thr75:.6f}")
else:
    thr75 = None

# -----------------------------
# 3) Chronological split (fractions)
#    Default: 70% train, 15% val, 15% test
# -----------------------------
train_frac, val_frac = 0.70, 0.15
n = len(df)
i_train = int(train_frac * n)
i_val   = int((train_frac + val_frac) * n)

train_df = df.iloc[:i_train].copy()
val_df   = df.iloc[i_train:i_val].copy()
test_df  = df.iloc[i_val:].copy()

# -----------------------------
# 4) Sanity checks + summary
# -----------------------------
TARGET = "badloan"  # change if your target is different

print("Final modelling frame shape:", df.shape)
print("Total missing values:", int(df.isna().sum().sum()))

# Regime share overall
if "stress_regime" in df.columns:
    print("\nStress regime share (overall):")
    print(df["stress_regime"].value_counts(normalize=True).rename("proportion").sort_index())

# Split summaries
summary = pd.DataFrame({
    "split": ["train", "val", "test"],
    **{k: [summarize_split(train_df, TARGET).get(k),
           summarize_split(val_df, TARGET).get(k),
           summarize_split(test_df, TARGET).get(k)]
       for k in ["n_rows","date_min","date_max","target_rate","stress_share","default_nonstress","default_stress"]}
})

print("\nSplit summary:")
display(summary)

# -----------------------------
# 5) Produce modelling matrices (only features + target)
#     (still no model training here)
# -----------------------------
# Keep target if present; drop internal helper cols
drop_cols = ["_time_"]
if time_col not in drop_cols:
    # keep time_col in df if you want, but for modelling you typically remove raw datetime
    pass

def make_xy(df_split, target_col=TARGET, time_col=time_col):
    df_split = df_split.copy()
    y = df_split[target_col].astype(int) if target_col in df_split.columns else None

    # Option A: drop raw datetime to avoid sklearn datetime errors
    X = df_split.drop(columns=[c for c in [target_col, "_time_", time_col] if c in df_split.columns], errors="ignore")

    return X, y

X_train, y_train = make_xy(train_df)
X_val,   y_val   = make_xy(val_df)
X_test,  y_test  = make_xy(test_df)

print("\nX/y shapes:")
print("X_train:", X_train.shape, "y_train:", y_train.shape)
print("X_val:  ", X_val.shape,   "y_val:  ", y_val.shape)
print("X_test: ", X_test.shape,  "y_test: ", y_test.shape)

# Optional: save splits for reproducibility
# train_df.to_parquet("train_split.parquet", index=False)
# val_df.to_parquet("val_split.parquet", index=False)
# test_df.to_parquet("test_split.parquet", index=False)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.compose import ColumnTransformer
from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import OneHotEncoder, StandardScaler, OrdinalEncoder
from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression, SGDClassifier
from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier
from sklearn.metrics import (
    roc_auc_score, average_precision_score,
    log_loss, brier_score_loss
)

# -----------------------------
# 0) Setup & Data Loading
# -----------------------------
TARGET = "badloan"
LEAK_COLS = [
    "loan_status", "loan_status_", "rankloan_status_",
    "out_prncp", "out_prncp_inv", "total_pymnt", "total_pymnt_inv",
    "total_rec_prncp", "total_rec_int", "total_rec_late_fee",
    "recoveries", "collection_recovery_fee", "last_pymnt_d",
    "last_pymnt_amnt", "next_pymnt_d", "last_credit_pull_d", "last_date"
]

def make_xy_optimized(df):
    # Drop leaks and datetimes in one go
    X = df.drop(columns=[TARGET] + [c for c in LEAK_COLS if c in df.columns], errors="ignore")
    dt_cols = X.select_dtypes(include=["datetime64[ns]", "datetime64[ns, UTC]"]).columns.tolist()
    X = X.drop(columns=dt_cols)
    y = df[TARGET].astype(int).values
    return X, y

X_train, y_train = make_xy_optimized(train_df)
X_val,   y_val   = make_xy_optimized(val_df)
X_test,  y_test  = make_xy_optimized(test_df)

cat_cols = X_train.select_dtypes(include=["object", "category"]).columns.tolist()
num_cols = [c for c in X_train.columns if c not in cat_cols]

# -----------------------------
# 1) Optimized Pipelines
# -----------------------------
# SPEED FIX: min_frequency groups rare categories to prevent "Feature Explosion"
preprocess_onehot = ColumnTransformer(
    transformers=[
        ("num", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median")),
            ("scaler", StandardScaler()) # Centering helps lbfgs converge faster
        ]), num_cols),
        ("cat", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="most_frequent")),
            ("onehot", OneHotEncoder(
                handle_unknown="ignore",
                min_frequency=0.01, # IMPORTANT: Groups rare labels into one column
                sparse_output=True
            ))
        ]), cat_cols),
    ],
    remainder="drop"
)

# HistGB prefers Ordinal; it's already very fast
preprocess_ordinal = ColumnTransformer(
    transformers=[
        ("num", Pipeline([("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median"))]), num_cols),
        ("cat", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="most_frequent")),
            ("ord", OrdinalEncoder(handle_unknown="use_encoded_value", unknown_value=-1))
        ]), cat_cols),
    ]
)

# -----------------------------
# 2) Faster Baseline Models
# -----------------------------
models = {
    "LogReg_L2": Pipeline([
        ("prep", preprocess_onehot),
        ("clf", LogisticRegression(
            max_iter=500,        # Sufficient for lbfgs
            solver="lbfgs",      # SPEED FIX: much faster than 'saga' for L2
            n_jobs=-1,
            class_weight="balanced"
        ))
    ]),
    "SGD_Log": Pipeline([
        ("prep", preprocess_onehot),
        ("clf", SGDClassifier(
            loss="log_loss",
            max_iter=100,
            tol=1e-3,
            class_weight="balanced",
            random_state=42
        ))
    ]),
    "HistGB": Pipeline([
        ("prep", preprocess_ordinal),
        ("clf", HistGradientBoostingClassifier(
            max_depth=6,
            max_iter=200, # Reduced slightly for baseline speed
            random_state=42
        ))
    ]),
}

# -----------------------------
# 3) Efficient Evaluation
# -----------------------------
def eval_model_fast(name, pipe, Xtr, ytr, Xv, yv, Xt, yt):
    print(f"Training {name}...")
    pipe.fit(Xtr, ytr)

    def get_p(X):
        return pipe.predict_proba(X)[:, 1] if hasattr(pipe, "predict_proba") else sigmoid(pipe.decision_function(X))

    # Evaluate Val and Test (Skip Train metrics to save time if needed)
    p_v = get_p(Xv)
    p_t = get_p(Xt)

    return {
        "model": name,
        "val_auc":   roc_auc_score(yv, p_v),
        "test_auc":  roc_auc_score(yt, p_t),
        "val_ap":    average_precision_score(yv, p_v),
        "test_ap":   average_precision_score(yt, p_t),
        "val_logloss": log_loss(yv, p_v),
        "test_brier":  brier_score_loss(yt, p_t),
    }, (p_v, p_t)

# -----------------------------
# 4) Execution
# -----------------------------
results = []
for name, pipe in models.items():
    metrics, _ = eval_model_fast(name, pipe, X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val, X_test, y_test)
    results.append(metrics)

results_df = pd.DataFrame(results).sort_values("val_auc", ascending=False)
display(results_df)

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sklearn.compose import ColumnTransformer
from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import OneHotEncoder, StandardScaler, OrdinalEncoder
from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer

from sklearn.linear_model import SGDClassifier
from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier

from sklearn.metrics import (
    roc_auc_score, average_precision_score,
    log_loss, brier_score_loss
)

# -----------------------------
# 0) Define target + features
# -----------------------------
TARGET = "badloan"

def make_xy(df, target=TARGET, drop_cols=None):
    df = df.copy()
    if drop_cols:
        df = df.drop(columns=[c for c in drop_cols if c in df.columns], errors="ignore")
    y = df[target].astype(int).to_numpy()
    X = df.drop(columns=[target], errors="ignore")
    return X, y

LEAK_COLS = [
    "loan_status", "loan_status_", "rankloan_status_",
    "out_prncp", "out_prncp_inv",
    "total_pymnt", "total_pymnt_inv", "total_rec_prncp", "total_rec_int",
    "total_rec_late_fee", "recoveries", "collection_recovery_fee",
    "last_pymnt_d", "last_pymnt_amnt", "next_pymnt_d", "last_credit_pull_d",
    "last_date"
]

X_train, y_train = make_xy(train_df, drop_cols=LEAK_COLS)
X_val,   y_val   = make_xy(val_df,   drop_cols=LEAK_COLS)
X_test,  y_test  = make_xy(test_df,  drop_cols=LEAK_COLS)

# -----------------------------
# 1) Remove datetime from inputs
# -----------------------------
datetime_cols = X_train.select_dtypes(include=["datetime64[ns]", "datetime64[ns, UTC]"]).columns.tolist()
X_train = X_train.drop(columns=datetime_cols, errors="ignore")
X_val   = X_val.drop(columns=datetime_cols, errors="ignore")
X_test  = X_test.drop(columns=datetime_cols, errors="ignore")

# -----------------------------
# 2) Column lists
# -----------------------------
cat_cols = X_train.select_dtypes(include=["object", "category"]).columns.tolist()
num_cols = [c for c in X_train.columns if c not in cat_cols]

# -----------------------------
# 3) Preprocess ONCE (cache matrices)
#    - min_frequency reduces huge one-hot blowups from rare categories
# -----------------------------
preprocess_onehot = ColumnTransformer(
    transformers=[
        ("num", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median")),
            ("scaler", StandardScaler(with_mean=False))
        ]), num_cols),
        ("cat", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="most_frequent")),
            ("onehot", OneHotEncoder(
                handle_unknown="ignore",
                sparse_output=True,
                min_frequency=50,   # key speed/memory win; adjust 20/50/100
                dtype=np.float32
            ))
        ]), cat_cols),
    ],
    remainder="drop",
    sparse_threshold=0.3
)

preprocess_ordinal = ColumnTransformer(
    transformers=[
        ("num", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median"))
        ]), num_cols),
        ("cat", Pipeline([
            ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="most_frequent")),
            ("ord", OrdinalEncoder(handle_unknown="use_encoded_value", unknown_value=-1))
        ]), cat_cols),
    ],
    remainder="drop"
)

print("Fitting one-hot preprocessor once...")
Xtr_oh = preprocess_onehot.fit_transform(X_train)
Xv_oh  = preprocess_onehot.transform(X_val)
Xt_oh  = preprocess_onehot.transform(X_test)

print("Fitting ordinal preprocessor once...")
Xtr_ord = preprocess_ordinal.fit_transform(X_train)
Xv_ord  = preprocess_ordinal.transform(X_val)
Xt_ord  = preprocess_ordinal.transform(X_test)

# -----------------------------
# 4) Models (baseline, scalable)
#    - Two logistic baselines trained with SGD (fast at 1.8M+)
#    - HistGB trained on subsample + early stopping (otherwise too slow)
# -----------------------------
def sigmoid(z):
    z = np.clip(z, -35, 35)
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z))

def get_proba_sgd(clf, X):
    # SGDClassifier with log_loss has predict_proba in newer sklearn, but keep fallback
    if hasattr(clf, "predict_proba"):
        return clf.predict_proba(X)[:, 1]
    return sigmoid(clf.decision_function(X))

def eval_probs(y_true, p):
    return {
        "auc": roc_auc_score(y_true, p),
        "ap": average_precision_score(y_true, p),
        "logloss": log_loss(y_true, p),
        "brier": brier_score_loss(y_true, p),
    }

results = []

# ---- Baseline 1: "LogReg" (SGD-trained logistic regression, L2)
logreg_sgd = SGDClassifier(
    loss="log_loss",
    penalty="l2",
    alpha=1e-5,
    max_iter=30,
    tol=1e-3,
    class_weight="balanced",
    random_state=42,
    average=True   # stabilises SGD, often improves calibration
)

print("Training: logreg_sgd_l2 (SGD logistic)...")
logreg_sgd.fit(Xtr_oh, y_train)

p_tr = get_proba_sgd(logreg_sgd, Xtr_oh)
p_v  = get_proba_sgd(logreg_sgd, Xv_oh)
p_t  = get_proba_sgd(logreg_sgd, Xt_oh)

m_tr = eval_probs(y_train, p_tr)
m_v  = eval_probs(y_val,   p_v)
m_t  = eval_probs(y_test,  p_t)

results.append({
    "model": "logreg_l2_sgd",
    "train_auc": m_tr["auc"], "val_auc": m_v["auc"], "test_auc": m_t["auc"],
    "train_ap":  m_tr["ap"],  "val_ap":  m_v["ap"],  "test_ap":  m_t["ap"],
    "train_logloss": m_tr["logloss"], "val_logloss": m_v["logloss"], "test_logloss": m_t["logloss"],
    "train_brier":   m_tr["brier"],   "val_brier":   m_v["brier"],   "test_brier":   m_t["brier"],
})

# ---- Baseline 2: SGD logistic (your "production" baseline, slightly different reg)
sgd_prod = SGDClassifier(
    loss="log_loss",
    penalty="l2",
    alpha=5e-6,      # slightly different regularisation than baseline 1
    max_iter=30,
    tol=1e-3,
    class_weight="balanced",
    random_state=42
)

print("Training: sgd_logloss...")
sgd_prod.fit(Xtr_oh, y_train)

p_tr = get_proba_sgd(sgd_prod, Xtr_oh)
p_v  = get_proba_sgd(sgd_prod, Xv_oh)
p_t  = get_proba_sgd(sgd_prod, Xt_oh)

m_tr = eval_probs(y_train, p_tr)
m_v  = eval_probs(y_val,   p_v)
m_t  = eval_probs(y_test,  p_t)

results.append({
    "model": "sgd_logloss",
    "train_auc": m_tr["auc"], "val_auc": m_v["auc"], "test_auc": m_t["auc"],
    "train_ap":  m_tr["ap"],  "val_ap":  m_v["ap"],  "test_ap":  m_t["ap"],
    "train_logloss": m_tr["logloss"], "val_logloss": m_v["logloss"], "test_logloss": m_t["logloss"],
    "train_brier":   m_tr["brier"],   "val_brier":   m_v["brier"],   "test_brier":   m_t["brier"],
})

# ---- Baseline 3: HistGB (subsample + early stopping)
#      (You can increase sample_n if it’s still fast enough)
sample_n = min(300_000, Xtr_ord.shape[0])
rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
idx = rng.choice(Xtr_ord.shape[0], size=sample_n, replace=False)

histgb = HistGradientBoostingClassifier(
    max_depth=6,
    learning_rate=0.05,
    max_iter=500,             # allow early stopping to decide
    early_stopping=True,
    validation_fraction=0.1,
    n_iter_no_change=10,
    random_state=42
)

print(f"Training: histgb (subsample n={sample_n})...")
histgb.fit(Xtr_ord[idx], y_train[idx])

p_tr = histgb.predict_proba(Xtr_ord[idx])[:, 1]     # train metrics on the same subsample
p_v  = histgb.predict_proba(Xv_ord)[:, 1]
p_t  = histgb.predict_proba(Xt_ord)[:, 1]

m_tr = eval_probs(y_train[idx], p_tr)
m_v  = eval_probs(y_val,        p_v)
m_t  = eval_probs(y_test,       p_t)

results.append({
    "model": f"histgb_sub{sample_n}",
    "train_auc": m_tr["auc"], "val_auc": m_v["auc"], "test_auc": m_t["auc"],
    "train_ap":  m_tr["ap"],  "val_ap":  m_v["ap"],  "test_ap":  m_t["ap"],
    "train_logloss": m_tr["logloss"], "val_logloss": m_v["logloss"], "test_logloss": m_t["logloss"],
    "train_brier":   m_tr["brier"],   "val_brier":   m_v["brier"],   "test_brier":   m_t["brier"],
})

results_df = pd.DataFrame(results).sort_values("val_auc", ascending=False)
display(results_df)

final_df = pd.read_parquet("/content/final_modelling_frame_with_split.parquet")

# 1) Split integrity
print(final_df["split"].value_counts())

# 2) Target sanity
print(final_df["badloan"].value_counts(normalize=True))

# 3) Leakage scan (critical)
print([c for c in final_df.columns if c.startswith(("loan_status", "rankloan_status", "loan_stat"))])

# 4) Chronological correctness
print(final_df.groupby("split")["issue_date"].agg(["min", "max"]))
